# Codex conversation 01a0e38d-3f8b-7ca3-a4fc-50da5ec41528

## assistant

I completed the signed-in AdeptLR and LSAT Demon audits and added them to the [running platform UI audit](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/research/2026-09-27-lsat-platform-ui-audit.md). It now compares all four competitors, including page layouts, navigation, practice setup, question review, small interaction details, and a ranked LSAT U plan.

**My strongest recommendation:** keep the shorter LSAT U rail we agreed on, then make Review the center of the learning loop. [LSAT Demon’s completed question review](https://lsatdemon.com/review/drill/52578486/test/141) keeps the original answer visible, reveals the correct choice deliberately, gives written reasons for each choice, and asks specific questions about the mistake. [AdeptLR’s builder](https://app.adeptlr.com/practice/create) adds two useful controls: pause for review and reveal the answer are separate choices, and the start button shows the available question count.

For the overall look, I recommend labeled navigation with short local tabs, a compact first screen, and a focused question workspace where the passage, answer state, and explanation stay together. LSAT U can go further by connecting Joshua’s assignment, the student’s attempt, his recap, and the next practice step. The audit specifies that layout and the implementation order. No new videos are needed.

I skipped Demon’s Admissions, Crushing 1L, and Tutor pages as requested. Demo and plan limits prevented verification of a new submitted answer, populated timed-test results, and paid class registration; those limits are marked in the log. No LSAT U code or live records were changed.

## developer

<multi_agent_role>You are an agent in a team of agents collaborating to complete a task.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

key: TabKey; label: string; Icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }> }[] = [\r\n  { key: \'bootcamps\', label: \'Bootcamps\', Icon: Flame },\r\n  { key: \'guided-drills\', label: \'Guided The tab is a real route segment (`/teacher/library/guided-drills`),\r\n * not a query param, so a tab is linkable, bookmarkable, and walks browser\r\n * history like every other room.\r\n *\r\n * The assign bundle lives here rather than in a tab, because its whole purpose\r\n * (spec D4) is to survive a tab change: a drill picked under Guided Drills and a\r\n * homework set picked under Homework ship to the student as one assignment.\r\n */\r\nexport function DeskLibrarySurface() {\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const { tab: tabParam } = useParams();\r\n  const tab: TabKey = isTab(tabParam) ? tabParam : DEFAULT_TAB;\r\n  const [view, setView] = useDeskLibraryView();\r\n\r\n  const [bundle, setBundle] = useState<AssignWorkSheetItem[]>([]);\r\n  // What the sheet is currently assigning: either a single item picked straight\r\n  // from a card, or the bundle. Null when the sheet is closed.\r\n  const [sheetItems, setSheetItems] = useState<AssignWorkSheetItem[] | null>(null);\r\n\r\n  const selectTab = (key: TabKey) => {\r\n    navigate(`/teacher/library/${key}`);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const addToBundle = useCallback((item: AssignWorkSheetItem) => {\r\n    setBundle((prev) =>\r\n      prev.some((p) => assignItemKey(p.item) === assignItemKey(item.item)) ? prev : [...prev, item],\r\n    );\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  const removeFromBundle = useCallback((item: AssignableItem) => {\r\n    setBundle((prev) => prev.filter((p) => assignItemKey(p.item) !== assignItemKey(item)));\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  const assignSingle = useCallback((item: AssignWorkSheetItem) => setSheetItems([item]), []);\r\n\r\n  const tabProps = { view, onAssign: assignSingle, onBundle: addToBundle };\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <section className="flex h-full w-full flex-col">\r\n      {/* Recessed onto a tinted band so it reads as a level below the room\r\n          rail in the shell header, not a second copy of it. */}\r\n      <nav className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-1 border-b border-black/10 bg-black/[0.045] px-4 py-1.5">\r\n        {TABS.map(({ key, label, Icon }) => (\r\n          <button\r\n            key={key}\r\n            type="button"\r\n            onClick={() => selectTab(key)}\r\n            className={cn(\r\n              \'inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition\',\r\n              tab === key\r\n                ? \'border-b-2 border-teacher-accent font-semibold text-black\'\r\n                : \'border-b-2 border-transparent text-black/55 hover:text-black\',\r\n            )}\r\n          >\r\n            <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> {label}\r\n          </button>\r\n        ))}\r\n        <div className="ml-auto">\r\n          <LibraryViewSwitcher value={view} onChange={setView} />\r\n        </div>\r\n      </nav>\r\n\r\n      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">\r\n        {tab === \'assignments\' && <DeskLibraryAssignmentsSurface view={view} />}\r\n        {tab === \'bootcamps\' && <DeskLibraryBootcampsSurface view={view} />}\r\n        {tab === \'guided-drills\' && <DeskLibraryGuidedDrillsSurface {...tabProps} />}\r\n        {tab === \'homework\' && <DeskLibraryHomeworkSurface {...tabProps} />}\r\n        {tab === \'activities\' && <DeskLibraryActivitiesSurface {...tabProps} />}\r\n        {tab === \'references\' && <DeskLibraryReferencesSurface />}\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      <AssignBundleBar\r\n        items={bundle}\r\n        onRemove={removeFromBundle}\r\n        onClear={() => setBundle([])}\r\n        onOpen={() => setSheetItems(bundle)}\r\n      />\r\n\r\n      <AssignWorkSheet\r\n        open={sheetItems !== null}\r\n        items={sheetItems ?? []}\r\n        onClose={() => setSheetItems(null)}\r\n        onAssigned={() => {\r\n          // Only the bundle is consumed by assigning it; a one-off assign from a\r\n          // card should not empty a bundle the teacher is still building.\r\n          if (sheetItems && sheetItems.length > 1) setBundle([]);\r\n        }}\r\n      />\r\n    </section>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport default DeskLibrarySurface;\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'RESULT 3\nimport { useMemo, useState } from \'react\';\r\nimport { NavLink, Outlet, useNavigate, useOutletContext, useParams } from \'react-router-dom\';\r\nimport { ArrowLeft, Activity, Plus } from \'lucide-react\';\r\nimport { format } from \'date-fns\';\r\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\r\nimport { useSelectedHubStudent, useStudentEmail, type HubStudentRow } from \'@/hooks/useHubStudents\';\r\nimport { formatLastActiveLabel, isUserOnline } from \'@/lib/presence\';\r\nimport AssignmentBuilder from \'@/components/teacher/AssignmentBuilder\';\r\nimport { cn } from \'@/lib/utils\';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The student dossier (spec T3.2): everything about one student behind one\r\n * URL, with the five surfaces as real child routes so each is linkable and\r\n * back/forward work. Replaces the split between TeacherStudentDetail, the\r\n * StudentHub page, and the roster\'s inline expander.\r\n */\r\n\r\nexport interface DossierContext {\r\n  studentId: string;\r\n  student: HubStudentRow | undefined;\r\n  studentName: string | null;\r\n  studentEmail: string | null;\r\n  /** Opens the shared assign flow already scoped to this student. */\r\n  openAssign: () => void;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function useDossier() {\r\n  return useOutletContext<DossierContext>();\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst TABS = [\r\n  { to: \'overview\', label: \'Overview\' },\r\n  { to: \'work\', label: \'Work\' },\r\n  { to: \'notes\', label: \'Notes\' },\r\n  { to: \'messages\', label: \'Messages\' },\r\n  { to: \'schedule\', label: \'Schedule\' },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport default function StudentDossier() {\r\n  const { studentId } = useParams<{ studentId: string }>();\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n\r\n  const student = useSelectedHubStudent(studentId ?? null);\r\n  // The roster query may not have resolved on a deep link, so fall back to the\r\n  // narrow per-student RPC rather than showing a nameless header.\r\n  const { data: rpcEmail } = useStudentEmail(studentId ?? null);\r\n\r\n  const [showAssign, setShowAssign] = useState(false);\r\n\r\n  const studentName = student?.display_name?.trim() || null;\r\n  const studentEmail = student?.email ?? rpcEmail ?? null;\r\n\r\n  const isOnline = isUserOnline(student?.last_seen_at ?? null);\r\n  const lastActiveLabel = formatLastActiveLabel(student?.last_seen_at ?? null);\r\n  const lastActiveAbsolute = student?.last_seen_at\r\n    ? format(new Date(student.last_seen_at), \'MMM d, h:mm a\')\r\n    : null;\r\n\r\n  const context = useMemo<DossierContext>(\r\n    () => ({\r\n      studentId: studentId as string,\r\n      student,\r\n      studentName,\r\n      studentEmail,\r\n      openAssign: () => setShowAssign(true),\r\n    }),\r\n    [student, studentEmail, studentId, studentName],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  if (!studentId) return null;\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <section className="flex h-full w-full flex-col" aria-label="Student dossier">\r\n      <header className="shrink-0 border-b border-black/10 px-5 pb-0 pt-4">\r\n        <button\r\n          type="button"\r\n          onClick={() => navigate(\'/teacher/students\')}\r\n          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-black/45 transition hover:text-black"\r\n        >\r\n          <ArrowLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n          Roster\r\n        </button>\r\n\r\n        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">\r\n          <div className="min-w-0">\r\n            <h1 className="truncate text-[20px] font-semibold tracking-tight text-black">\r\n              {studentName || \'Student\'}\r\n            </h1>\r\n            <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-black/45">\r\n              {studentEmail && <span className="truncate font-mono">{studentEmail}</span>}\r\n              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">\r\n                <Activity className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n                {isOnline ? \'Online on LSAT U\' : \'Offline\'}\r\n              </span>\r\n              <span>{lastActiveLabel}</span>\r\n              {lastActiveAbsolute && <span>· {lastActiveAbsolute}</span>}\r\n            </div>\r\n          </div>\r\n\r\n          <button\r\n            type="button"\r\n            onClick={() => setShowAssign(true)}\r\n            className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 bg-teacher-accent px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-teacher-accent-hover"\r\n          >\r\n            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={2} />\r\n            Assign work\r\n          </button>\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        {/* Sub-tabs sit below the shell\'s room rail, so they take the ramp\'s\r\n            13px label step — subordinate to the rail\'s 14px without inventing\r\n            an undocumented size. */}\r\n        <nav className="-mb-px mt-3 flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto" aria-label="Dossier sections">\r\n          {TABS.map((tab) => (\r\n            <NavLink\r\n              key={tab.to}\r\n              to={tab.to}\r\n              className={({ isActive }) =>\r\n                cn(\r\n                  \'whitespace-nowrap border-b-2 px-3 py-2 text-[13px] transition\',\r\n                  isActive\r\n                    ? \'border-black font-semibold text-black\'\r\n                    : \'border-transparent font-medium text-black/45 hover:text-black/75\',\r\n                )\r\n              }\r\n            >\r\n              {tab.label}\r\n            </NavLink>\r\n          ))}\r\n        </nav>\r\n      </header>\r\n\r\n      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto">\r\n        <Outlet context={context} />\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      {showAssign && user && (\r\n        <AssignmentBuilder\r\n          studentId={studentId}\r\n          adminUserId={user.id}\r\n          onClose={() => setShowAssign(false)}\r\n        />\r\n      )}\r\n    </section>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n'}]

Line 208, position 45.\r\n# Teacher Desk (page) — Context\r\n\r\nThe glossary for the Teacher Desk **page** itself (`/teacher`, the `desk/` surface): the named visual regions of the page and what each one is for. Scoped narrower than the teacher-workspace glossary ([../CONTEXT.md](../CONTEXT.md)), which defines the workspace\'s *concepts* (Today, the task modes, the AI Teaching Assistant, the pipeline). This file names the *boxes on the screen* so we can talk about changing them without ambiguity. It is a glossary, not a spec.\r\n\r\nThe page-level concept (what the desk is *for*) lives in the parent glossary under **Teacher Desk** and **Today**. This file does not restate that; it names the regions.\r\n\r\n> **Update (chat-first desk):** the left rail\'s **Tools** section (Reply/Review/Assign mode list + Next cards) has been replaced by a ChatGPT-style saved-chats rail. The desk now reads: Workspace options → **New chat** → **Saved chats** (`DeskChatRail`); the center is a light assistant transcript + composer (`DeskChatPanel`); the right rail is a light task/to-do list (`DeskTodoRail`). The chat session is shared via `OperatorChatProvider`.\r\n>\r\n> Superseded (kept on disk, no longer imported, pending a cleanup decision): `ModeList.tsx`, `DeskCube.tsx`, `WorkSurface.tsx`, `ActionContextPanel.tsx`, `MiniChat.tsx`. The region glossary below still describes the pre-chat task surface for reference.\r\n\r\n## Region glossary\r\n\r\n### Desk Header\r\nThe top metadata line of the page. Contains exactly three things:\r\n- **Operating day** (left): the weekday + date Joshua is operating on (e.g. "Thu — 18 June").\r\n- **Status slot** (right): one contextual readout — "Preview data" when signed out locally, otherwise the **next-session cue** (the upcoming session), or empty when nothing is cued.\r\n- **Close** (far right): leaves the desk and returns to `/mainhub`.\r\n\r\nBorrowed from the instrument-panel reference\'s top metadata line. It is a readout + exit, not a place Joshua does teaching work.\r\n\r\n### Workspace Tabs\r\nThe left-side global navigation labeled WORKSPACE: the top-level Teacher Workspace **areas** — the big places Joshua goes. These are deliberately distinct from the **Task Modes** (Reply/Review/Assign), which live *inside* the Today/Desk page. A Workspace Tab is a destination you leave the desk for; a Task Mode is work you do on the desk. No tab may duplicate a mode. (Renders as a vertical rail today; "tabs" is the chosen product term.)\r\n\r\n**Ratified tab set (4):**\r\n- Today → `/teacher` (the desk itself)\r\n- People → `/teacher?ws=people`\r\n- Inbox → `/teacher/conversations`\r\n- Library → `/teacher/library`\r\n- Student detail routes such as `/teacher/students/:studentId` remain deep profile routes, but the list/admin workspace is People.\r\n\r\nRemoved from the prior set:\r\n- **Assign** — deleted as a tab. It duplicated **Assign mode** and made the page confusing; Assign now lives *only* as a desk Task Mode.\r\n- **Teach** (`/teacher/whiteboard`) — dropped as a top-level tab. The whiteboard surface still exists and **moves under Library** (alongside assignments, recaps, activities). It is reached via Library, not the rail.\r\n\r\nThe old separate Students/Admin rail split was collapsed into People so roster context and access controls live together.\r\n\r\n### Task Modes\r\nThe left-side list of the three Today task modes, each with a live count of items waiting. Selecting a mode changes what the Stage shows. These are the "Today Task Groups" from the parent glossary, rendered as the desk\'s mode selector.\r\n\r\nThe three modes are always named **Reply mode**, **Review mode**, and **Assign mode** — never "Reply task," "task group," or bare "Reply." The word "mode" is part of the canonical name.\r\n\r\n**Prominence + label:** today this list is labeled "TOOLS," which both mislabels it (these are modes, not tools) and buries it. It must be the **clearest, most prominent thing** in the left sidebar — it is how Joshua chooses what he\'s working on. Relabel the section to name what it is (e.g. "MODES" / "TODAY"); do not call it "Tools." Since Assign is no longer a Workspace Tab, this is the *only* place Assign mode is reachable, so it cannot be visually secondary.\r\n\r\n### Next Move\r\nThe left-side block labeled NEXT: the single suggested next teacher action (or "No current teacher move"). Whatever it surfaces is always a Reply-, Review-, or Assign-mode task. Sourced from `useNextSession`.\r\n\r\n### Desk Cube\r\nThe centered wireframe cube plus the CURRENT MODE label naming the active mode (Reply / Review / Assign mode). Its only job is to show which mode is active; it carries no data the teacher acts on. (Tension to resolve later: the parent glossary\'s instrument-panel direction warns against "decorative cube visuals that do not serve teaching work.")\r\n\r\n### To-Do Rail\r\nThe right column (`DeskTodoRail`): a light task/to-do list. **Today-scoped** — it belongs to the Today workspace only and must not render on other Workspace Tabs (Library, Students, Admin, Inbox). When a non-Today tab is active, the rail is absent and the desk content area takes its width. (Earlier behavior rendered it on every workspace; that was the bug.)\r\n\r\n### Stage\r\nThe center column: the region that holds the currently selected task and the means to act on it. Today it contains a task card (title + preview) and the MiniChat beneath it. The shape of the Stage is the main thing under redesign.\r\n\r\n### Assistant Chat\r\nThe AI Teaching Assistant (parent glossary) rendered as a chat inside the Stage. Today it contains: a transcript, the Draft Box, and a terminal-style command input. It stays inside the desk shell and never becomes a full-page chat. (Was called "MiniChat"; renamed because it is becoming the primary content of the Stage, not a mini add-on.)\r\n\r\n### Draft Bubble\r\nReplaces the old Draft Box. A **draft** is a piece of student-facing content the assistant produces (a reply, recap, or assignment message). It renders inside the Assistant Chat as a **distinct full-width document card** — NOT a chat speech bubble. It carries a header strip (draft-type label on the left, **Edit** + **Send** controls on the right) so it reads as an artifact you will send, visually separate from ordinary AI chatter.\r\n\r\n- **Resting state.** Before any draft exists there is **no draft box at all** — the Stage shows the task card + the chat transcript + the Composer, nothing else. The old always-on empty white textarea is killed. (Decision: "Nothing but the composer.")\r\n- **Appears only on `create_draft`.** The card shows up only when the assistant produces a draft. Ordinary assistant chatter — advice, clarifying questions — stays a plain left-aligned chat message with no Edit/Send.\r\n- **Form.** Full-width card, header strip (`✦ DRAFT REPLY` style label + Edit/Send), body is the draft text. Plain chatter stays small bubbles; the draft card is deliberately heavier and wider so "this is the thing that goes to the student" is unmistakable.\r\n- **Edit** makes the card body directly editable (rich text: bold, italic, links). Selecting text inside it pops an **Ask for Changes** toolbar, so Joshua can hand-edit or ask the AI to revise just that selection.\r\n- **Send** lives on the card. Sending commits that exact text to the student. Because Send is per-card, the old right-panel Commit Button is **retired** for the draft modes (Reply, Review).\r\n\r\n**Revision lifecycle.** Each AI revision is a new card. Only the **newest** card is full-size and editable; **older revisions collapse to a one-line chip** (`› v1 · superseded`) that can be expanded. This keeps the canvas from growing unbounded while preserving history. (Decision: "Collapse old, latest live.")\r\n\r\n**Send behavior — confirm step.** Send is a two-tap commit: the first tap turns the button into **`Send to {student}?`** with confirm / cancel for ~3s; the second tap commits. On commit the card **locks to a receipt** — `✓ Sent to Maria · 2:14pm`, body no longer editable. This guards against misfiring student-facing messages. " classroom-account-savestate-visible" : ""}`}\r\n              aria-live="polite"\r\n            >\r\n              {saving ? (\r\n              <button\r\n                type="button"\r\n                className="classroom-account-ghost-button"\r\n                onClick={() => void commit(defaultRailPrefs())}\r\n                disabled={saving || loading}\r\n              >\r\n                <RotateCcw size={12} aria-hidden="true" />\r\n                Reset to default\r\n              </button>\r\n            ) : null}\r\n          </div>\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        {groups.map((group) => {\r\n          const ids = group.items.map((i) => i.id as string);\r\n          return (\r\n            <section className="classroom-account-card" key={group.id}>\r\n              <header className="classroom-account-card-head">\r\n                <h2>{group.title}</h2>\r\n              </header>\r\n              <ul className="classroom-account-rows">\r\n                {group.items.map((item, idx) => {\r\n                  const isHidden = hiddenSet.has(item.id);\r\n                  return (\r\n                    <li className="classroom-account-row" key={item.id}>\r\n                      <span className="classroom-account-row-arrows">\r\n                        <button\r\n                          type="button"\r\n                          onClick={() => move(group.id, ids, idx, -1)}\r\n                          disabled={idx === 0 || saving}\r\n                          aria-label={`Move ${item.label} up`}\r\n                        >\r\n                          <ChevronUp size={14} aria-hidden="true" />\r\n                        </button>\r\n                        <button\r\n                          type="button"\r\n                          onClick={() => move(group.id, ids, idx, 1)}\r\n                          disabled={idx === ids.length - ## assistant

Current LSAT U navigation supports the proposed consolidation:

| Proposed area | Existing surface |
|---|---|
| Home | [ClassroomHomeView.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/components/classroom/home/ClassroomHomeView.tsx:138) already shows next work, notes, sessions, and a seven-day view. |
| My Work | Assigned-work section in [ClassroomLayout.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/components/classroom/shell/ClassroomLayout.tsx:524); teacher dossier Work is already a tab. |
| Practice | Separate Drill, Testing Centre, saved sets, and bootcamp routes in [App.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/App.tsx:430). |
| Review | Wrong answers, flagged questions, analytics, and history are separate rail items in [railCatalog.ts](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/railCatalog.ts:56). |
| Library | Teacher Library already uses linkable tabs for Bootcamps, Guided Drills, Homework, Assignments, Activities, and References in [DeskLibrarySurface.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/pages/teacher/desk/DeskLibrarySurface.tsx:22). |
| Schedule | Student Schedule and Booking share one rail item; teacher scheduling lives in each student dossier. |
| Teacher workspace | Four top tabs, Today, Inbox, Library, Insights, in [TeacherWorkspaceShell.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/pages/teacher/shell/TeacherWorkspaceShell.tsx:28). Student dossiers already have Overview, Work, Notes, Messages, and Schedule tabs in [StudentDossier.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/pages/teacher/students/StudentDossier.tsx:32). |

The student rail is driven by [railCatalog.ts](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/railCatalog.ts:7); users can reorder or hide its items in [RailLayoutEditor.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/components/classroom/account/RailLayoutEditor.tsx:20). Consolidation must preserve saved preferences, existing deep links, and feature gates. [railCatalog.test.ts](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/railCatalog.test.ts:55) explicitly checks rail-to-route permissions.

The existing code deliberately separates timed Testing Centre from Drill to prevent accidental timer starts. A unified Practice page should retain visibly distinct Drill and Timed tabs with an explicit start action.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'RESULT 0\n.classroom-root {\r\n  --classroom-line: #f3e3d6;\r\n  /* The boundary of a control, not of a surface. --classroom-line is a hairline\r\n     for separating things that are already identifiable; where the border IS\r\n     what tells you a region is clickable, WCAG 1.4.11 asks for 3:1 and the\r\n     hairline measures 1.22:1 against the page. Same warm family, darkened only\r\n     in value: 3.14:1 on the panel and 3.06:1 on the page. */\r\n  --classroom-line-strong: #9c8f85;\r\n  /* --classroom-line-strong is measured against the page and the panel. A control\r\n     that sits on a tinted ground instead (the toggle knob, on --classroom-hover)\r\n     reads under 3:1 there, so it takes this one. Same role, darker ground. */\r\n  --classroom-line-on-tint: #8a7d72;\r\n  /* The switch\'s on-track. Light is --classroom-active unchanged; dark is a\r\n     lighter sibling of it, because --classroom-active over the dark panel left\r\n     off-vs-on at 1.51:1 and knob position was carrying the state alone. Scoped\r\n     to the switch so the other 17 --classroom-active rules keep their colour. */\r\n  --classroom-toggle-on-track: #43403c;\r\n  --classroom-ink: #43403c;\r\n  --classroom-ink-body: #43403c;\r\n  --classroom-meta: 166, 162, 158;\r\n  --classroom-hover: rgba(246, 235, 225, 0.4);\r\n  --classroom-dash: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15);\r\n  --classroom-link: #ff8906;\r\n  /* Text-safe pair. --classroom-link and --classroom-meta are tuned for fills,\r\n     rules and decorative use, where WCAG 1.4.11\'s 3:1 does not apply. Against\r\n     white they measure 2.38:1 and 2.54:1, so they fail 1.4.3 as TEXT.\r\n     Use these two instead whenever the colour carries words or notation.\r\n     Same hue (32deg / 30deg), darkened only in value: 5.39:1 and 5.38:1 on\r\n     white, and >=4.5:1 on every 10% tint used in the classroom. */\r\n  --classroom-link-text: #a25704;\r\n  --classroom-meta-text: #6d6a67;\r\n  /* The unread badge carries a number, so it is text and owes 4.5:1. The\r\n     orange that works as a fill does not survive white on top of it: white on\r\n     --classroom-link measures 2.38:1. Each theme therefore picks the pairing\r\n     its own ground allows rather than sharing one. Light darkens the fill and\r\n     keeps white on it (5.39:1); dark keeps the bright fill and puts the page\'s\r\n     near-black on it (8.06:1). */\r\n  --classroom-accent: #a25704;\r\n  --classroom-accent-ink: #ffffff;\r\n  /* Kept as aliases so the badge rule reads in its own terms. */\r\n  --classroom-badge-fill: var(--classroom-accent);\r\n  --classroom-badge-ink: var(--classroom-accent-ink);\r\n  /* The panel/card ground, so a token that names a surface actually resolves. */\r\n  --classroom-surface: #ffffff;\r\n  --classroom-active: #43403c;\r\n 300ms;\r\n  align-items: flex-start;\r\n  background: var(--classroom-page);\r\n  color: var(--classroom-ink);\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  font-family: "Outfit", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;\r\n  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;\r\n  font-weight: 500;\r\n  min-height: 100dvh;\r\n  transition:\r\n    background-color var(--classroom-transition) ease,\r\n    color var(--classroom-transition) ease;\r\n  width: 100%;\r\n}\r\n\r\nhtml.dark .classroom-root {\r\n  --classroom-line: #17161c;\r\n  /* 3.21:1 on the dark page, 3.31:1 on the dark panel. */\r\n  --classroom-line-strong: #646176;\r\n  /* Lighter, not darker: on the dark theme the tinted ground is the lighter one. */\r\n  --classroom-line-on-tint: #7a7789;\r\n  --classroom-toggle-on-track: #6f6d80;\r\n  --classroom-ink: #fffffe;\r\n  --classroom-ink-body: #e5e5e5;\r\n  --classroom-meta: 167, 169, 190;\r\n  --classroom-hover: rgba(68, 64, 60, 0.4);\r\n  --classroom-dash: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15);\r\n  --classroom-active: #3d3c48;\r\n  --classroom-active-ink: #e5e5e5;\r\n  --classroom-meta-soft: #a7a9be;\r\n  /* On the dark page both base values already clear 8:1, so the text-safe pair\r\n     is the same colour here. Declared so the token means one thing everywhere. */\r\n  --classroom-link-text: #ff8906;\r\n  --classroom-meta-text: #a7a9be;\r\n  --classroom-accent: #ff8906;\r\n  --classroom-accent-ink: #0f0e17;\r\n  --classroom-badge-fill: var(--classroom-accent);\r\n  --classroom-badge-ink: var(--classroom-accent-ink);\r\n  --classroom-surface: #0a0913;\r\n  --classroom-panel: #0a0913;\r\n  --classroom-page: #0f0e17;\r\n  --classroom-drill-page: #121019;\r\n  --classroom-trigger: #0a0913;\r\n  --classroom-wrong: #fb7185;\r\n  --classroom-unsure: #fbbf24;\r\n  --classroom-correct: #34d399;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root *,\r\n.classroom-root *::before,\r\n.classroom-root *::after {\r\n  box-sizing: border-box;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* Split for the same reason the colour reset below is written with :where().\r\n   `font: inherit` at (0,1,1) outranks every Tailwind font utility (0,1,0), so\r\n   a button carrying `text-[13px] font-black` inside this frame rendered at the\r\n   inherited 16px/500 instead — which is what the Messages stage is full of.\r\n   Family and style stay at reset strength (that is the part a reset is for:\r\n   form controls otherwise default to Arial); the metrics a component may\r\n   legitimately state for itself drop to (0,0,1), so a bare control still\r\n   inherits and a stated size, weight, or leading now wins. */\r\n.classroom-root button,\r\n.classroom-root input,\r\n.classroom-root textarea {\r\n  font-family: inherit;\r\n  font-style: inherit;\r\n  font-variant: inherit;\r\n}\r\n\r\n:where(.classroom-root) :is(button, input, textarea) {\r\n  font-size: inherit;\r\n  font-weight: inherit;\r\n  line-height: inherit;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* A reset, so it must carry a reset\'s specificity. Written plainly as\r\n   `.classroom-root button` it scores (0,1,1) and outranks every Tailwind text\r\n   utility (0,1,0), which silently swallowed the colour of any button that set\r\n   its own: `text-white` on the hero\'s PrepTest trigger rendered near-black on\r\n   the near-black console (~1:1 contrast), and `text-primary-foreground` on the\r\n   launchers\' filled CTAs rendered white on white. `:where()` contributes zero\r\n   specificity, dropping this to (0,0,1): bare buttons still inherit, and any\r\n   button that states a colour now keeps it. */\r\n:where(.classroom-root) button {\r\n  color: inherit;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-panel-wash {\r\n  border-radius: inherit;\r\n  inset: 0;\r\n  overflow: hidden;\r\n  pointer-events: none;\r\n  position: absolute;\r\n  z-index: 0;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-panel-wash i {\r\n  display: block;\r\n  inset: 0;\r\n  position: absolute;\r\n  transition: opacity var(--classroom-transition) ease;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-panel-wash-light {\r\n  background-image: linear-gradient(\r\n    161.15deg,\r\n    rgba(254, 252, 247, 0.4) 12.73%,\r\n    rgba(243, 227, 214, 0.4) 72.95%\r\n  );\r\n  opacity: 1;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-panel-wash-dark {\r\n  background-image: linear-gradient(\r\n    161.15deg,\r\n    rgba(41, 37, 36, 0.4) 12.73%,\r\n    rgba(68, 64, 60, 0.4) 72.95%\r\n  );\r\n  opacity: 0;\r\n}\r\n\r\nhtml.dark .classroom-root .classroom-panel-wash-light {\r\n  opacity: 0;\r\n}\r\n\r\nhtml.dark .classroom-root .classroom-panel-wash-dark {\r\n  opacity: 1;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-panel-inner {\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  flex-direction: column;\r\n  min-height: 100%;\r\n  position: relative;\r\n  z-index: 1;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-sidebar {\r\n  flex: 0 0 336px;\r\n  height: 100dvh;\r\n  padding: 8px;\r\n  position: sticky;\r\n  top: 0;\r\n  width: 336px;\r\n  z-index: 20;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-sidebar-card {\r\n  background: var(--classroom-panel);\r\n  border: 1px solid var(--classroom-line);\r\n  border-radius: var(--classroom-radius);\r\n  height: 100%;\r\n  overflow: auto;\r\n  padding: 24px;\r\n  position: relative;\r\n  transition:\r\n    background-color var(--classroom-transition) ease,\r\n    border-color var(--classroom-transition) ease;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-brand {\r\n  align-items: center;\r\n  background: transparent;\r\n  border: 0;\r\n  cursor: pointer;\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  gap: 9px;\r\n  margin: 0 0 24px;\r\n  padding: 0;\r\n  text-align: left;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-brand i {\r\n  align-items: center;\r\n  background: var(--classroom-ink);\r\n  border-radius: 6px;\r\n  color: var(--classroom-panel);\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  font-size: 9px;\r\n  font-style: normal;\r\n  font-weight: 700;\r\n  height: 22px;\r\n  justify-content: center;\r\n  width: 22px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-brand span,\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-nav-group > b,\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-profile-copy small,\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-row-status,\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-section-meta,\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-message time,\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-note-meta {\r\n  font-family: "IBM Plex Mono", ui-monospace, "SF Mono", monospace;\r\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\r\n  font-weight: 500;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-brand span {\r\n  letter-spacing: 0.04em;\r\n  text-transform: uppercase;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-nav-group {\r\n  margin-bottom: 18px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-nav-group > b {\r\n  color: var(--classroom-meta-text);\r\n  display: block;\r\n  letter-spacing: 0.04em;\r\n  margin-bottom: 6px;\r\n  padding: 0 8px;\r\n  text-transform: uppercase;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-nav {\r\n  align-items: center;\r\n  background: transparent;\r\n  border: 0;\r\n  border-radius: var(--classroom-radius);\r\n  cursor: pointer;\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  font-size: 14px;\r\n  gap: 8px;\r\n  line-height: 1.45;\r\n  padding: 7px 8px;\r\n  text-align: left;\r\n  text-decoration: none;\r\n  /* 14px x 1.45 + 7px x 2 came to 34px. The rail is already flex-centred, so a\r\n     min-height gives a real target rather than one faked with padding. */\r\n  transition:\r\n    background-color var(--classroom-transition) ease,\r\n    color var(--classroom-transition) ease;\r\n  width: 100%;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-nav:hover {\r\n  background: var(--classroom-hover);\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-nav-locked,\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-nav-locked:hover {\r\n  background: transparent;\r\n  color: var(--classroom-meta-text);\r\n  cursor: default;\r\n  opacity: 0.7;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-nav-lock {\r\n  flex: 0 0 auto;\r\n  height: 13px;\r\n  margin-left: auto;\r\n  width: 13px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-nav-active {\r\n  background: var(--classroom-active);\r\n  color: var(--classroom-active-ink);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* Whiteboard rail button: white pill instead of the rail\'s usual dark\r\n   active fill, so it doesn\'t read as another dark nav item. */\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-nav-active-white {\r\n  background: var(--classroom-surface);\r\n  color: var(--classroom-ink);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* Unread count on a rail item. Sits where the `em` chevron sits on the section\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "RESULT 1\n   ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\r\n.drill-observatory-launcher-panel {\r\n  --background: 240 6% 7%;\r\n  --foreground: 40 20% 96%;\r\n  --card: 240 6% 10%;\r\n  --card-foreground: 40 20% 96%;\r\n  --popover: 240 6% 9%;\r\n  --popover-foreground: 40 20% 96%;\r\n  --primary: 40 20% 96%;\r\n  --primary-foreground: 240 6% 7%;\r\n  --secondary: 240 5% 16%;\r\n  --secondary-foreground: 40 20% 96%;\r\n  --muted: 240 5% 15%;\r\n  --muted-foreground: 40 8% 68%;\r\n  --accent: 240 5% 18%;\r\n  --accent-foreground: 40 20% 96%;\r\n  --border: 0 0% 100% / 0.12;\r\n  --input: 0 0% 100% / 0.14;\r\n  --ring: 40 20% 80%;\r\n\r\n  color: hsl(var(--foreground));\r\n}\r\n\r\n\r\n/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\n   Messages stage\r\n\r\n   Messages is reached from the rail's Support group and must stay inside this\r\n   frame — routing it out swapped the whole rail mid-navigation. Unlike the\r\n   reading surfaces it is a chat: it needs the full height beside the rail, not\r\n   the 720px centred column, so it gets its own stage and no section list.\r\n   ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\r\n.classroom-root.classroom-root-messages {\r\n  height: 100dvh;\r\n  overflow: hidden;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-messages-stage {\r\n  flex: 1 1 auto;\r\n  height: 100dvh;\r\n  min-width: 0;\r\n  overflow: hidden;\r\n}\r\n\r\n@media (max-width: 1023px) {\r\n  /* Clear the fixed menu button, the same way .classroom-column does. */\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-messages-stage {\r\n    padding-top: 62px;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\n   Teacher workspace stage\r\n\r\n   The rail opens the workspace INTO this stage rather than navigating away\r\n   from the classroom frame. The workspace draws its own room rail and header,\r\n   so it needs the full height beside the classroom rail with no reading\r\n   column and no section list — the same shape Messages uses.\r\n   ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\r\n.classroom-root.classroom-root-workspace {\r\n  height: 100dvh;\r\n  overflow: hidden;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-workspace-stage {\r\n  flex: 1 1 auto;\r\n  height: 100dvh;\r\n  min-width: 0;\r\n  overflow: hidden;\r\n}\r\n\r\n@media (max-width: 1023px) {\r\n  /* Clear the fixed menu button, the same way .classroom-column does. */\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-workspace-stage {\r\n    padding-top: 62px;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\n   Account stage\r\n\r\n   Native classroom surfaces for the Profile and Customize-layout tabs. All\r\n   controls are hand-styled on classroom tokens (no shadcn) so dark mode\r\n   follows the classroom palette, not the broken global .dark variables.\r\n   ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-account-grid {\r\n  align-items: flex-start;\r\n  display: grid;\r\n  gap: 16px;\r\n  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 240px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n@media (max-width: 1023px) {\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-account-grid {\r\n    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);\r\n  }\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-account-side {\r\n    display: none;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-account-main {\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  flex-direction: column;\r\n  gap: 14px;\r\n  min-width: 0;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-account-card {\r\n  background: var(--classroom-panel);\r\n  border: 1px solid var(--classroom-line);\r\n  border-radius: var(--classroom-radius);\r\n  padding: 16px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-account-card-head {\r\n  margin-bottom: 10px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-account-card-head h2 {\r\n  color: var(--classroom-meta-text);\r\n  font-size: 11px;\r\n  font-weight: 600;\r\n  letter-spacing: 0.08em;\r\n  margin: 0;\r\n  text-transform: uppercase;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-account-card-foot {\r\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--classroom-line);\r\n  margin-top: 12px;\r\n  padding-top: 12px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* Identity */\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-account-identity {\r\n  align-items: flex-start;\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  gap: 14px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-account-avatar {\r\n  flex: 0 0 auto;\r\n  font-size: 16px;\r\n  height: 44px;\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'RESULT 2\nImport-Clixml: Root element is missing.\r\nInvalidOperation: Index operation failed; the array index evaluated to null.\r\nInvalidOperation: Index operation failed; the array index evaluated to null.\r\n  position: fixed;\r\n  inset: 0;\r\n  z-index: 60;\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  align-items: center;\r\n  justify-content: center;\r\n  overflow-y: auto;\r\n  padding: clamp(1.5rem, 6vw, 5rem) clamp(1.25rem, 6vw, 4rem);\r\n  background: var(--classroom-page);\r\n  animation: section-briefing-in 320ms cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.7, 0.3, 1) both;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-inner {\r\n  width: 100%;\r\n  max-width: 46rem;\r\n  margin: auto;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-eyebrow {\r\n  display: block;\r\n  font-size: 0.6875rem;\r\n  font-weight: 600;\r\n  letter-spacing: 0.24em;\r\n  text-transform: uppercase;\r\n  color: var(--classroom-link-text);\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-head h1 {\r\n  margin: 0.9rem 0 0;\r\n  font-size: clamp(2.25rem, 7vw, 3.75rem);\r\n  line-height: 1.02;\r\n  letter-spacing: -0.022em;\r\n  color: var(--classroom-ink);\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-lede {\r\n  margin: 1rem 0 0;\r\n  max-width: 34rem;\r\n  font-size: clamp(1rem, 2.4vw, 1.125rem);\r\n  line-height: 1.6;\r\n  color: var(--classroom-meta-text);\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-cols {\r\n  display: grid;\r\n  gap: clamp(1.75rem, 5vw, 3rem);\r\n  margin-top: clamp(2.25rem, 6vw, 3.25rem);\r\n  padding-top: clamp(2.25rem, 6vw, 3.25rem);\r\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--classroom-line);\r\n}\r\n\r\n@media (min-width: 46rem) {\r\n  .section-briefing-cols {\r\n    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-cols h2 {\r\n  margin: 0 0 1rem;\r\n  font-size: 0.6875rem;\r\n  font-weight: 600;\r\n  letter-spacing: 0.2em;\r\n  text-transform: uppercase;\r\n  color: var(--classroom-meta-text);\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-cols ul {\r\n  margin: 0;\r\n  padding: 0;\r\n  list-style: none;\r\n  display: grid;\r\n  gap: 0.85rem;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-cols li {\r\n  position: relative;\r\n  padding-left: 1.1rem;\r\n  font-size: 0.9375rem;\r\n  line-height: 1.55;\r\n  color: var(--classroom-ink-body);\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-cols li::before {\r\n  content: "";\r\n  position: absolute;\r\n  left: 0;\r\n  top: 0.62em;\r\n  width: 5px;\r\n  height: 1px;\r\n  background: var(--classroom-link-text);\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-actions {\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\r\n  align-items: center;\r\n  gap: 1.5rem;\r\n  margin-top: clamp(2.25rem, 6vw, 3.25rem);\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-start {\r\n  padding: 0.95rem 2.75rem;\r\n  border: 0;\r\n  border-radius: 999px;\r\n  background: var(--classroom-link-text);\r\n  color: #fff;\r\n  font-size: 0.9375rem;\r\n  font-weight: 600;\r\n  letter-spacing: 0.02em;\r\n  cursor: pointer;\r\n  transition: transform 160ms ease, filter 160ms ease;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-start:hover {\r\n  filter: brightness(1.06);\r\n  transform: translateY(-1px);\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-start:focus-visible {\r\n  outline: 2px solid var(--classroom-ink);\r\n  outline-offset: 3px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-later {\r\n  border: 0;\r\n  background: none;\r\n  padding: 0;\r\n  font-size: 0.875rem;\r\n  color: var(--classroom-meta-text);\r\n  cursor: pointer;\r\n  text-underline-offset: 4px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.section-briefing-later:hover {\r\n  color: var(--classroom-ink);\r\n  text-decoration: underline;\r\n}\r\n\r\n@keyframes section-briefing-in {\r\n  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(10px); }\r\n  to   { opacity: 1; transform: none; }\r\n}\r\n\r\n@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {\r\n  .section-briefing { animation-duration: 1ms; }\r\n  .section-briefing-start { transition: none; }\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* ── Touch targets ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\n   `.classroom-nav` computes to 34px, and the same class draws the mobile\r\n   drawer, where it is a genuine touch target rather than a mouse one. So the\r\n   44px floor is applied where a finger is doing the pointing.\r\n\r\n   This is not a deviation, despite an earlier note here that said it was.\r\n   PRODUCT.md\'s binding floor is WCAG 2.1 AA. The 44x44 figure is 2.5.5, which\r\n   is Level AAA; the AA target-size rule is 2.2\'s 2.5.8, and it asks 24x24. A\r\n   34px row clears the binding floor by ten pixels. Applying 44px globally would\r\n   add ~170px of height to a 17-row rail to satisfy a criterion the product has\r\n   not adopted.\r\n\r\n   PRODUCT.md line 104 lists the AAA number inside its AA section. That is worth\r\n   correcting in the doc; it is not a defect in this file. */\r\n@media (pointer: coarse) {\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-nav,\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-profile-menu-item {\r\n    min-height: 44px;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-mobile-menu .classroom-nav {\r\n  min-height: 44px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* ── The Notes and recaps landing ──────────────────────────────────────────\r\n   The stage carried a dateline, an <h1> and a one-line count into a 720px\r\n   column, so the widest region of the page held the least information. These\r\n   rules dress the lede and the featured note that now fill it.\r\n\r\n   No card. The stage has no container around it, and wrapping the only content\r\n   on the page in a border would be a box for its own sake. Hierarchy comes from\r\n   size, weight and space, and separation from a single hairline. */\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-surface-lede {\r\n  color: var(--classroom-meta-text);\r\n  font-size: 15px;\r\n  line-height: 1.6;\r\n  margin: -8px 0 32px;\r\n  max-width: 52ch;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.classroom-root .classroom-featured-note {\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'RESULT 3\n/* Main Conclusion bootcamp scoped styles (active only inside .mc-bootcamp). */\r\n@import "./bootcamps/main-conclusion/styles/scoped.css";\r\n\r\n@tailwind base;\r\n@tailwind components;\r\n@tailwind utilities;\r\n\r\n/* Definition of the design system. All colors, gradients, fonts, etc should be defined here.\r\nAll colors MUST be HSL.\r\n*/\r\n\r\n@layer base {\r\n  :root {\r\n    color-scheme: dark;\r\n\r\n    /* LSAT U — Dark-first design system.\r\n       Tokens resolve to the dark palette by default.\r\n       Add class="light" to <html> to switch to light mode.\r\n    */\r\n    --background: 0 0% 3.9%;         /* neutral-950 */\r\n    --foreground: 0 0% 98%;           /* near white */\r\n\r\n    --card: 0 0% 7%;                  /* neutral-900 equivalent */\r\n    --card-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\r\n\r\n    --popover: 0 0% 7%;\r\n    --popover-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\r\n\r\n    --primary: 0 0% 98%;              /* white — primary interactive on dark */\r\n    --primary-foreground: 0 0% 3.9%; /* dark text behind white elements */\r\n\r\n    --secondary: 0 0% 14.9%;          /* neutral-800 */\r\n    --secondary-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\r\n\r\n    --muted: 0 0% 14.9%;\r\n    --muted-foreground: 0 0% 63.9%;   /* neutral-500 */\r\n\r\n    --accent: 0 0% 14.9%;\r\n    --accent-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\r\n\r\n    /* Status colors */\r\n    --success: 142 71% 45%;\r\n    --success-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\r\n\r\n    --warning: 38 92% 50%;\r\n    --warning-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\r\n\r\n    --destructive: 0 72% 51%;\r\n    --destructive-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\r\n\r\n    /* Attempt-state semantic tokens.\r\n       Used by drill / flashcard / playground feedback across topic bootcamps.\r\n       Hue-distinct from --success / --destructive so attempt feedback never\r\n       collides visually with system status. */\r\n    --state-correct: 160 84% 39%; The bar is a third child, so\r\n     in a row it claimed a column of its own and squeezed the stage (271px of\r\n     375 on a phone). Below the sidebar breakpoint the room stacks instead, and\r\n     the bar is a full-width band above the stage. */\r\n  .classroom-root {\r\n    align-items: stretch;\r\n    flex-direction: column;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-sidebar {\r\n    display: none;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  /* The bar takes 56px of the flow, so a full-height stage would push every\r\n     short page 56px past the viewport. */\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-stage,\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-stage-pending {\r\n    min-height: calc(100dvh - 56px);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-mobile-bar {\r\n    align-items: center;\r\n    background: var(--classroom-page);\r\n    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--classroom-line);\r\n    display: flex;\r\n    /* A column flex item shrinks when its siblings ask for the full height, and\r\n       Messages did exactly that: the bar came out 52px instead of 56. */\r\n    flex: none;\r\n    gap: 12px;\r\n    height: 56px;\r\n    padding: 0 12px;\r\n    position: sticky;\r\n    top: 0;\r\n    z-index: 70;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  .classroom-root.classroom-root-drill .classroom-mobile-bar {\r\n    background: var(--classroom-drill-page);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-mobile-menu-button {\r\n    display: flex;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  /* Was 72px, the clearance the fixed button needed. The bar has its own\r\n     space now, so the column only needs its ordinary inset. */\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-column {\r\n    padding: 16px 0 90px;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  .classroom-root.classroom-root-schedule .classroom-column {\r\n    padding: 16px 0 72px;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-leader-row {\r\n    align-items: flex-start;\r\n    flex-direction: column;\r\n    gap: 2px;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-dotted-leader {\r\n    display: none;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  .classroom-root .classroom-covering-link {\r\n    min-height: 100%;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  .classroom-root.classroom-root-journal-detail {\r\n'}]

## assistant

The active student rail is 336px wide. Its optional second list is another 336px, so both together consume 672px before the main content. The second list hides below 1280px, or 1400px in the journal; the rail becomes a 56px mobile header and drawer below 1024px. See [classroom.css](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/components/classroom/classroom.css:194) and its [responsive rules](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/components/classroom/classroom.css:3310).

The default content column is 720px; Home expands to 1260px and Schedule to 1240px. Classroom styling uses Outfit, small IBM Plex Mono navigation labels, warm white surfaces, dark charcoal text, and an orange accent. Teacher screens use a separate light gray surface and amber accent. These values are defined in [classroom.css](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/components/classroom/classroom.css:1) and [index.css](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/index.css:99).
